concurrency 104, EAGLE3 steps 3 and draft 4, FP8 KV and page size 64,

memory fraction 0.90 and explicitly request 16384 KV tokens. The earlier
5811.19 run profiled 17152 tokens with hot-4 weights; this leaves 768 tokens
or 12 pages of headroom while preserving the actual-capacity guard.

at most B22 or M88 including transfer slots. Do not retune shapes or kernels.

retain shared ordered overlay staging and the CPU Enroot mount preflight,

budget plus the previous 620-second ASPIRE run estimates 14m52s total.
Even twice that added budget estimates 19m24s. H100 timing remains to be
measured by the parent session.
